diff --git a/README.md b/README.md
index 76ed8710189..9cd9e839204 100644
--- a/README.md
+++ b/README.md
@@ -252,6 +252,8 @@ The deep loops run unattended and stop only when their own evidence says the wor
 
 Each pass runs in a fresh context against state kept on disk. A script, not the agent, decides when to stop. A review stays open while a P0 is active or a required check still fails, and it ends in a PASS, CONDITIONAL or FAIL verdict. A loop survives crashes and new sessions, because its progress lives in files.
 
+Two opt-in flags let a loop reach further. `--depth-mode=expansive` keeps a frontier of sub-questions or sub-targets and turns a stop into another pass while one stays open, though never past the iteration cap. `--extend=N` adds N passes to a finished run in the same session. A review can also track custom dimensions beside its four defaults.
+
 A loop can also spread across several AI CLIs at once. Each executor runs its own lineage, and for a review the strictest verdict wins the merge. A pool caps how many lineages run at once. A stall watchdog stops a lineage that stops making progress, and a cost cap bounds what each one spends. The [Deep Loop README](.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/README.md) describes both modes and the shared runtime.

#!/usr/bin/env node

// ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// MODULE: Deep-Loop Convergence Entrypoint
// ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

// The stop decision is computed from loop state rather than from prose, so two
// runs over the same state stop at the same point. Its object owns nine fields
// in this order: decision, stopReason, reasonDetail, weightedStopScore, signals,
// gates, blockedBy, graphDecision and inputs. stopReason keeps the frozen
// eight-value set; the finer cause travels in reasonDetail as
// maxIterationsReached, consecutiveFailures, stuckThresholdReached,
// convergenceModeOff, stopPolicyMaxIterations, noStopCandidate,
// minIterationsNotReached, frontierOpen,
// qualityGuardFailed, all_questions_answered, all_dimensions_clean,
// composite_converged, graphStopBlocked, graph_convergence_continue,
// graphDecisionMissing, or the name of a failed gate.

'use strict';

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// 1. IMPORTS
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

const fs = require('node:fs');
const path = require('node:path');
const { spawnSync } = require('node:child_process');

const {
  acquireWriterLock,
  assertKnownFlags,
  classifyExitCode,
  installSignalHandlers,
  maybeThrowTestFault,
  validateNamespaceValue,
} = require('./lib/cli-guards.cjs');

const {
  appendObservabilityEvent,
} = require('../lib/deep-loop/observability-events.cjs');

              Options:
              A) Retry: dispatch the next iteration with the same focus
              B) Skip: dispatch the next iteration with the next focus
              C) Cancel: end the run with no synthesis

No service-tier.

Off-allowlist model or missing binary fails closed — a requested CLI executor never degrades to native.

**Phase Output:**
- `research_topic` | `spec_choice` | `spec_path`
- `execution_mode` | `maxIterations` | `convergenceThreshold` | `convergence_mode` | `depth_mode`

## 2. Dashboard / Checkpoint Layout

### Deep Research

Conduct autonomous iterative deep research with convergence detection. Each iteration dispatches a fresh LEAF agent (`@deep-research`) that reads externalized state, performs focused investigation, and writes findings to files.

For code review and quality auditing, see `/deep:review`.

### Convergence Threshold Semantics

**Default:** 0.05 on newInfoRatio (fully-new=1.0, partially-new=0.5, +0.10 simplicity bonus, capped 1.0)

**Semantic:** `convergenceThreshold` compares newly discovered information against accumulated research knowledge with negative-knowledge emphasis. Lower = more iterations / higher signal threshold.

**NOT INTERCHANGEABLE with siblings:**
- `deep-review` uses 0.10 default on weighted P0/P1/P2 severity ratio

96:**Lifecycle policy:** `lineage_mode=auto` preserves the existing classifier behavior, `lineage_mode=resume` continues a valid packet, and `lineage_mode=restart` archives the resolved `{artifact_dir}` before any config write, phase init, or fan-out spawn. In `:auto`, an explicit `--restart` or `--lineage-mode=restart` flag is operator authorization for this archive move, so do not ask for a second confirmation. Preserve rollback by moving the timestamped archive back to `review/` if needed. A restart request must never silently downgrade to resume; if the archive step cannot run, halt with `STATUS=FAIL`.
97-
98-**Stop policy:** `stop_policy=convergence` preserves normal deep-review behavior: legal convergence may stop before `maxIterations`. `stop_policy=max-iterations` makes convergence signals telemetry-only until the iteration ceiling; before `maxIterations`, broaden the next angle instead of synthesizing. Terminal manual pause, user stop, unrecoverable error, and `maxIterationsReached` still stop.
--
127:   |-- --restart -> lineage_mode = restart
128:   |-- --lineage-mode=auto|resume|restart -> lineage_mode = value
129:   |-- --extend=N -> extend = N, lineage_mode = completed-continue (positive integer only; a completed run only; refused with fan-out)
130:   |-- --extend-minutes=M -> extend_minutes = M (positive integer only; valid only with --extend)
131-   |-- --executor=<type> -> config.executor.kind (`native` | `cli-codex` | `cli-claude-code` | `cli-opencode` | `cli-cursor` | `cli-devin` | `cli-pi` | `cli-deepseek-harness` | `cli-mistral-vibe`. Every kind runs in `:auto` and `:confirm`, `cli-deepseek-harness` as a single lineage or a fan-out and `cli-mistral-vibe` only as a fan-out lineage. `EXECUTOR_KINDS` in `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/lib/deep-loop/executor-config.ts` is authoritative)

## 2. HOW IT WORKS

The live initialization contract classifies the packet state before it writes anything. It distinguishes fresh state, resumable state, completed-session state, and invalid state. Fresh runs create `research/deep-research-config.json`, the first JSONL line in `research/deep-research-state.jsonl`, `research/deep-research-strategy.md`, and `research/findings-registry.json`. Confirm mode adds a charter review gate before the first iteration continues.

Lineage handling is narrower than some older drafts. The runtime supports `new`, `resume`, `restart`, and `completed-continue`. Resume keeps the same session identifier and appends a typed `resumed` event. Restart archives the old research tree, mints a fresh session identifier, increments generation, and appends a typed `restarted` event. `completed-continue` is live through `--extend=N` on a completed run: it keeps the session identifier and generation, sets `maxIterations` to the iterations run so far plus N, appends a typed `run_extended` event, and copies the prior synthesis to a versioned snapshot before the first new iteration.

`fork` remains deferred and is not exposed as a live mode.

---

## 3. SOURCE FILES

### Implementation
=== loop-protocol 130-142

#### Completed-run extension

`--extend=N` (and optionally `--extend-minutes=M`) continues a run whose config has `status: "complete"`. Under the loop lock and before session classification, `step_extend_completed_run`:

1. Counts `continuedFromRun` as the distinct runs among the iteration records, error and timeout rows included.
2. Sets `maxIterations` to `continuedFromRun + N`, so a run stopped at 7 of 10 and extended by 3 ends at 10.
3. Sets `maxDurationMinutes` to `max(prior budget, minutes elapsed since createdAt) + M`. `M` defaults to the prior budget, and a run with no duration budget keeps none unless `--extend-minutes` is passed. `createdAt` is never re-stamped, because the duration check counts wall-clock time, idle time included.
4. Appends `deep_research.run_extended` through the append gateway, which projects an `extended` row into the state log, and only then replaces the config with `status: "initialized"`, `lineageMode: "completed-continue"` and `continuedFromRun`. A failed append leaves the run complete and untouched.

Classification then lands on resume. The workflow writes no `resumed` row for an extension, and the reducers treat an `extended` row like `resumed`, so the run is live again. Before the first new dispatch, `step_snapshot_extended_synthesis` copies the prior synthesis to `research/synthesis-v{generation}-r{continuedFromRun}.md` and refuses to overwrite an existing snapshot, so a second extension of the same generation keeps its own copy. `--extend` refuses a run that is not complete (use `--lineage-mode=resume`), refuses fan-out before any lineage spawns, and accepts positive integers only.

The extended row carries `{"type":"event","event":"extended","sessionId","generation","lineageMode":"completed-continue","continuedFromRun","extension","timestamp"}`, where `extension` holds `addedIterations`, `priorMaxIterations`, `newMaxIterations`, `priorMaxDurationMinutes`, `newMaxDurationMinutes` and `elapsedMinutesAtExtension`. It has no `archivedPath` or `parentSessionId`, so the field rule below covers `resumed` and `restarted` only.

[0m$ [0mcd /Users/michelkerkmeester/worktrees/public/455-expansive-depth-integration && sed -n '1,100p' .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/tests/unit/stop-decision-yaml-contract.vitest.ts
// ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// MODULE: Stop Decision YAML Contract
// ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { createRequire } from 'node:module';
import { resolve } from 'node:path';

// The stop decision is computed by a script from the state files. A workflow
// step that still carries vote arithmetic would let two runs over the same
// state stop at different points, so the step may only branch on the script's
// stop_decision object.
const ASSETS = resolve(__dirname, '..', '..', '..', '..', '..', 'commands', 'deep', 'assets');

// js-yaml is a dependency of the shared package the runtime declares, so the
// require is anchored there rather than at a package this runtime never names.
const sharedRequire = createRequire(resolve(__dirname, '..', '..', '..', '..', 'system-spec-kit', 'shared', 'package.json'));
const { load: parseYaml } = sharedRequire('js-yaml') as { load: (source: string) => unknown };
const FILES = [
  'deep-research.yaml',
  'deep-review.yaml',
];
const FORBIDDEN_IN_STEP = [
  'MAD(',
  'mean(last',
  'w=0.',
  '0.08',
  '0.05',
  'checkQualityGuards',
  'graph_decision is absent',
  'Skip hard stops',
];

function stepText(text: string, step: string): string {
  const start = text.indexOf(`      ${step}:\n`);
  if (start === -1) return '';
  const rest = text.slice(start + 1);
  const end = rest.search(/\n      step_[a-z_]+:\n/);
  return end === -1 ? rest : rest.slice(0, end);
}

describe.each(FILES)('%s', (file) => {
  const text = readFileSync(resolve(ASSETS, file), 'utf8');

  it('step_check_convergence holds no vote arithmetic', () => {
    const step = stepText(text, 'step_check_convergence');
    expect(step.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    const found = FORBIDDEN_IN_STEP.filter((fragment) => step.includes(fragment));
    expect(found).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('step_check_convergence branches on stop_decision', () => {
    const step = stepText(text, 'step_check_convergence');
    expect(step).toContain('stop_decision.decision');
    expect(step).toContain('stop_decision.stopReason');
  });

  it('runs convergence.cjs once with --stop-decision', () => {
    const occurrences = text.split('runtime/scripts/convergence.cjs').length - 1;
    expect(occurrences).toBe(1);
    const invocation = text
      .split('\n')
      .find((line) => line.includes('runtime/scripts/convergence.cjs')) ?? '';
    expect(invocation).toContain('--stop-decision');
  });

  it('the divergent pivot reads stop_decision.reasonDetail', () => {
    const pivot = text
      .split('\n')
      .filter((line) => line.includes('branch_on') && line.includes('stop_decision.reasonDetail'));
    expect(pivot.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });

  it('hands the divergent pivot the fine cause, not the coarse stop reason', () => {
    const lines = text
      .split('\n')
      .filter((line) => line.includes('--trigger-reason "{reason_detail}"'));
    expect(lines.length).toBe(1);
  });

  if (file.startsWith('deep-review')) {
    it('holds no hardcoded 0.05 threshold', () => {
      expect(text).not.toMatch(/0\.05\b/);
    });
  }
});

// The opt-in depth mode must be bound everywhere the convergence mode is, so an
// unbound value can never reach convergence.cjs as an empty argument.
describe('deep-research binds depth_mode beside convergence_mode', () => {
  const yaml = readFileSync(resolve(ASSETS, 'deep-research.yaml'), 'utf8');
  const presentation = readFileSync(resolve(ASSETS, 'deep-research-presentation.txt'), 'utf8');

  it('declares depth_mode in user_inputs and requires it in the preflight contract', () => {
    expect(yaml).toMatch(/\n  depth_mode: "\[default|expansive\]/);
    expect(yaml).toContain('convergence_mode, depth_mode, stop_policy]');
  });

title: "DRV-072 -- Review run holds below its minIterations floor"

# DRV-072 -- Review run holds below its minIterations floor

This document captures the realistic user-testing contract, current behavior, execution flow, source anchors, and metadata for `DRV-072`.

---

## 1. OVERVIEW

This scenario validates the minimum-iteration floor for `DRV-072`. It focuses on a review run whose stop candidate arrives before its configured `minIterations`: the convergence check must return `CONTINUE` with reason `minIterationsNotReached`, and the run may release only once the floor is reached.

### Why This Matters

The floor used to apply to research runs only. A review that reached a stop candidate at iteration 2 with a floor of 3 could stop one iteration early, before the reviewer had the second full pass the floor exists to guarantee. The shipped floor of 2 is unchanged, so only an operator who sets a higher floor sees the change.

---

## 2. SCENARIO CONTRACT

Operators run this as a single check of the convergence decision with a floor set above the stop candidate, so the outcome comes from the floor and not from the default.

- Objective: Confirm that a review stop candidate below `minIterations` returns `CONTINUE` with `minIterationsNotReached`, and that the same run releases at the floor.
- Real user request: I set a review to run at least three iterations. Do not let it stop at two, even if it looks converged.
- Prompt: `Stop a deep-review run that reaches a stop candidate at iteration 2 with minIterations set to 3, and confirm the run continues with minIterationsNotReached and releases only at iteration 3.`
- Expected execution process: Read the floor check in the convergence script, read the stop-order item in the review workflow, then run the convergence and stop-decision tests that drive a real review loop state through the floor.
- Expected signals: The floor check returns `CONTINUE` with reason `minIterationsNotReached` at iteration 2, the same loop state releases at iteration 3, the shipped floor of 2 still passes its replay, the review workflow names the floor as stop-order item `4a`, and the stop-decision case reads that a review run gets the floor.
- Desired user-visible outcome: The user is told the review will not stop before its configured minimum, and why it continued.
- Pass/fail: PASS if the iteration-2 candidate continues with `minIterationsNotReached`, the iteration-3 candidate releases, and the replay and parity suites pass; FAIL if a review releases below its floor or the floor applies to research only.

---

## 3. TEST EXECUTION

### Prompt

======

- Prompt: `Stop a deep-review run that reaches a stop candidate at iteration 2 with minIterations set to 3, and confirm the run continues with minIterationsNotReached and releases only at iteration 3.`

### Commands

1. `rg -n 'minIterationsNotReached' .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/scripts/convergence.cjs`
2. `rg -n '4a\. if iteration_count' .skilled/commands/deep/assets/deep-review.yaml`
3. `cd .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime && npx vitest run review-depth-convergence.vitest stop-decision.vitest review-stop-path-replay.vitest stop-policy-yaml-parity.vitest`

### Expected

Command 1 prints the floor's `CONTINUE` return with `minIterationsNotReached`. Command 2 prints stop-order item `4a`. Command 3 exits 0 with no failed test. The `review-depth-convergence` case shows `CONTINUE` at iteration 2 and a release at iteration 3 from the same loop state.

### Evidence

Capture the command 1 and 2 matches, and the command 3 summary with the named cases.

### Pass / Fail

- **Pass**: The iteration-2 candidate returns `CONTINUE` with `minIterationsNotReached`, the iteration-3 candidate releases, the replay still passes at the shipped floor, and the YAML parity suite passes.
- **Fail**: A review releases below its floor, the reason is missing or wrong, the replay fails at the shipped floor, or the stop-order item is absent from the workflow.

### Failure Triage

If the run releases at iteration 2, check that the floor check in `convergence.cjs` runs on the candidate before the legal-stop gates and no longer depends on the loop type. If the shipped replay fails, the floor value has changed, since the shipped floor of 2 must stay as it is.

### Optional Supplemental Checks

- Shipped floor: run the stop-path replay with its fixtures unchanged and confirm the outcome matches its recorded expectation.

        action: "Branch on the stop decision that convergence.cjs computed in step_graph_convergence"
        algorithm: |
          The whole stop decision is computed by convergence.cjs from the review config, the state log, the findings registry and the coverage graph. This step performs no arithmetic and never recomputes or overrides the result.
          The ratio signals leave out thought iterations and every iteration whose status is error or timeout, and stuck_count is loop_counters_json.stuckCount.
          The script applies this fixed order, first match wins:
          1. if iteration_count >= max_iterations: STOP (maxIterationsReached). This terminal cap is evaluated before every veto, the claim-adjudication veto included; failed legal-stop gates are recorded in stop_decision.blockedBy as synthesis evidence.
          1a. After 3 consecutive failed iterations (status error or timeout), halt the loop and enter synthesis with stop reason error and partial findings.
          2. if stuck_count >= config.stuckThreshold: STUCK_RECOVERY
          3. if convergence_mode == "off": skip convergence stop candidates and continue.
          3a. if stop_policy == "max-iterations" AND iteration_count < max_iterations: the vote is telemetry only and the decision is CONTINUE.
          4. a STOP candidate exists when every dimension is clean (all_dimensions_clean) or the weighted vote passes (composite_converged). The vote cutoffs follow the configured convergenceThreshold.
          4a. if iteration_count < min_iterations: CONTINUE (minIterationsNotReached). min_iterations is config.minIterations, else antiConvergence.minIterations, capped at max_iterations; like research, the floor runs on a candidate before the legal-stop gates.
          5. the nine legal-stop gates run on a candidate: convergenceGate, dimensionCoverageGate, p0ResolutionGate, evidenceDensityGate, hotspotSaturationGate, claimAdjudicationGate, fixCompletenessReplayGate, candidateCoverageGate, graphlessFallbackGate. Any failed gate gives BLOCKED (blockedStop).

---
title: "DRV-070 -- Completed-run extension with --extend"
description: "This scenario validates `--extend=N` on a completed deep-review run for `DRV-070`. It focuses on same-session continuation, the `run_extended` ledger event and the review report snapshot taken before re-synthesis."
version: 1.0.2.0
---

# DRV-070 -- Completed-run extension with --extend

This document captures the realistic user-testing contract, current behavior, execution flow, source anchors, and metadata for `DRV-070`.

---

## 1. OVERVIEW

This scenario validates completed-run extension for `DRV-070`. It focuses on `--extend=N` reopening a completed review run in the same session and generation, recording the extension as one `run_extended` event, and keeping the prior review report as a versioned snapshot before the run re-synthesizes.

### Why This Matters

A review that stopped at its iteration cap often has one more dimension worth checking. The operator should be able to add N iterations to that same review without a new session. If the extension resets the generation, drops the prior report, or appends a second extension event, the review history no longer shows what was checked and when.

---

## 2. SCENARIO CONTRACT

Operators run this as a real orchestrator-led check against a completed fixture review rather than a synthetic matrix. The scenario is only complete when the operator can explain, in plain language, how many iterations the extension added and where the prior report was kept.

- Objective: Verify that `--extend=3` on a completed review run adds exactly three iterations in the same session and generation, records one `run_extended` event, and snapshots the prior review report.
- Real user request: The review finished at its iteration cap, and one dimension still has open questions. Give the same review three more iterations.
- Prompt: `Extend the completed deep-review run in the fixture spec folder by three iterations with --extend=3, then confirm the original session identifier, the single run_extended event and the review report snapshot.`
- Expected execution process: Read the completed fixture config under `{artifact_dir}`, run the review command with `--extend=3` against that spec folder, then read the state log, the config and the snapshot after the run finishes.
- Expected signals: Exactly one `run_extended` event appears, projected as one `extended` row in `deep-review-state.jsonl`. The config keeps the original `sessionId` and generation 1, `maxIterations` equals the prior iteration count plus 3, and the lineage mode is `completed-continue`. No `resumed` row is written for the extension. A file matching `review-report-v*-r*.md` holds the prior report, and exactly three new iteration files are written under `iterations/`.
- Desired user-visible outcome: The user is told the review was extended by three iterations, that the same session continues, and where the prior report was kept.
- Pass/fail: PASS if the event count, the session identifier, the iteration cap, the snapshot and the iteration count all match the expected signals; FAIL if any one of them differs, including a second `run_extended` event or a missing snapshot.

---

## 3. TEST EXECUTION

### Prompt

- Prompt: `Extend the completed deep-review run in the fixture spec folder by three iterations with --extend=3, then confirm the original session identifier, the single run_extended event and the review report snapshot.`

### Commands

1. `rg -n -- '--extend' .skilled/commands/deep/review.md .skilled/commands/deep/assets/deep-review.yaml`
2. `rg -n 'run_extended' .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/lib`
3. `cd .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime && npx vitest run append-mode-event-legacy-seam.vitest workflow-restart-lifecycle.vitest`
4. `/deep:review "<fixture target>" :auto --spec-folder=<completed-fixture-spec-folder> --extend=3`
5. `rg -c '"event":"extended"' <artifact_dir>/deep-review-state.jsonl` (the `run_extended` stem lives in the ledger, and the state log carries its projected `extended` row)
6. `jq '{sessionId, generation, maxIterations, lineageMode}' <artifact_dir>/deep-review-config.json`
7. `find <artifact_dir> -name 'review-report-v*-r*.md'`
8. `ls <artifact_dir>/iterations | wc -l`

`<artifact_dir>` is the review artifact folder under the fixture spec folder, the folder that holds `deep-review-config.json`.

### Expected

Command 1 prints the `--extend` binding and the extension step in both files. Command 2 prints the `run_extended` stem and its payload type. Command 3 exits 0 with no failed test. After command 4 finishes, command 5 prints `1`, command 6 shows the original `sessionId`, generation 1, `maxIterations` at the prior count plus 3 and `lineageMode` `completed-continue`, command 7 lists one snapshot file, and command 8 shows three more iteration files than before the run.

### Evidence
=== cat
---
title: "Completed-run extension (--extend)"
description: "A completed deep-review run extends in place by N iterations under --extend=N, keeps its session identifier and generation, and keeps the prior review report as a snapshot before re-synthesis."
trigger_phrases:
  - "--extend"
  - "--extend=N review"
  - "extend completed review run"
  - "review run_extended"
  - "--extend-minutes review"
version: 1.0.1.0
---

# Completed-run extension (--extend)

## 1. OVERVIEW

`--extend=N` reopens a completed review run in the same session and generation and adds `N` iterations. The run keeps its `sessionId` and generation, records one `run_extended` ledger event, and keeps the prior review report as `review/review-report-v{generation}-r{continuedFromRun}.md` before re-synthesis. `--extend-minutes=M` adds minutes to the duration budget of the same extension.

---

## 2. HOW IT WORKS

### Lineage And Preconditions

The extension uses lineage mode `completed-continue`. The run must already be complete. An unfinished review continues with `--lineage-mode=resume` instead. The extension is refused with fan-out.

### Iteration And Duration Budget

The iteration cap rises by `N` over the prior count. `--extend-minutes=M` is valid only with `--extend`. The new duration budget is `max(prior, elapsed) + M`, because the clock counts time since `createdAt`.

### Stop Interaction

Under `stopPolicy: convergence`, an extension with nothing new can stop again at once on `all_dimensions_clean`. Pass `--convergence-mode=off` or use `stopPolicy: max-iterations`.

### Ledger And Reducer Routing

The `run_extended` stem is part of the deep-review ledger schema, and the deep-review reducer routes it. `runtime/scripts/reduce-state.cjs` reads the extension through its live lineage modes.

---
[0m$ [0mcd /Users/michelkerkmeester/worktrees/public/455-expansive-depth-integration && sed -n '440,560p' .skilled/commands/deep/assets/deep-review.yaml
- "{state_paths.legacy_root_report}"
          legacy_review_markers:

      step_acquire_lock:
        action: "Acquire the single-writer advisory lock before session classification"
        dry_run_boundary: "state-mutation"
        ttl_ms: "lock_ttl_ms = max(1800000, executor_timeout * 1000). executor_timeout is the bound dispatch timeout in seconds, 900 when unset. The lock is stale after twice this value, so it outlives the longest allowed dispatch."
        command: "node .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/scripts/loop-lock.cjs acquire --lock-path {state_paths.lock_file} --packet-id {spec_folder} --ttl-ms {lock_ttl_ms}"
        on_acquire: "Capture lock.ownerPid as captured_owner_pid and lock.acquireNonce as captured_acquire_nonce from the JSON output, and reuse both for every release below. If acquired=false, fail closed — another deep-review run already holds this packet. Under confirm, mode_overrides.confirm.lock_not_acquired applies instead."
        note: "Uses the promoted runtime/ loop-lock CLI (single source of truth: stale-lock reclaim, clean owner-scoped release). macOS/BSD locking is advisory — best-effort single-writer, not a hard mutex across host calls. Acquire reclaims a stale lock itself and reports the old holder under `reclaimed`. A lock is stale when its heartbeat is older than twice its TTL, or when its owning process is known to be dead. No confirmation step exists in either mode: log a `reclaimed` holder and continue."
        # Lock-cleanup contract: the lock file MUST be released on every terminal exit path that follows acquisition.
        # Runtimes honoring step_acquire_lock are required to perform this cleanup before halting or pausing.
        on_halt:
          action: "Every terminal halt after the acquire runs step_release_lock before it returns control"
          command: "node .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/scripts/loop-lock.cjs release --lock-path {state_paths.lock_file} --owner-pid {captured_owner_pid} --nonce {captured_acquire_nonce}; failures here are logged but do not block the halt"
        on_cancel:
          action: "Release the lock on user cancel or pause-sentinel detection before sentinel processing"

        on_workflow_exit:
          action: "Release the lock as the final step of any workflow exit path (success or failure)"
          command: "node .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/scripts/loop-lock.cjs release --lock-path {state_paths.lock_file} --owner-pid {captured_owner_pid} --nonce {captured_acquire_nonce}; idempotent — safe to invoke when already released"

      step_extend_completed_run:
        action: "Extend a completed run in place, under the lock and before classification, so the classifier sees a resumable run"
        skip_when: "lineage_mode != 'completed-continue'"
        dry_run_boundary: "state-mutation"
        note: |
          --extend=N continues a finished run in the same session and generation. The
          duration clock counts wall-clock time since createdAt, idle time included, so the
          new budget starts from whichever is later, the old budget or the time already
          spent, and createdAt is never re-stamped. The event is appended before the

          node -e '
            const fs = require("fs"), crypto = require("crypto"), path = require("path");
            const [configPath, stateLogPath, extendRaw, extendMinutesRaw, workDir] = process.argv.slice(1);
            const fail = (message) => { console.error(message); process.exit(2); };
            const config = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(configPath, "utf8"));
            // The preflight check refuses these first; this keeps a bad value from reaching the config.
            if (!/^[1-9][0-9]*$/.test(String(extendRaw))) {
              fail("--extend needs a positive integer, and got " + JSON.stringify(String(extendRaw)) + ".");
            }
            const minutesRaw = String(extendMinutesRaw);
            if (minutesRaw !== "" && !minutesRaw.startsWith("{") && !/^[1-9][0-9]*$/.test(minutesRaw)) {
              fail("--extend-minutes needs a positive integer, and got " + JSON.stringify(minutesRaw) + ".");
            }
            if (config.status !== "complete") {
              fail("--extend needs a completed run, and this run has status " + config.status + ". Use --lineage-mode=resume to continue an unfinished run.");
            }
            const stateBytes = fs.readFileSync(stateLogPath);
            const rows = stateBytes.toString("utf8").split("\n").filter(Boolean).flatMap((line) => {
              try { return [JSON.parse(line)]; } catch { return []; }
            });
            // One run per iteration number, error and timeout rows included. The integer
            // iteration counts first and a numeric run is the fallback, as the state reader
            // counts them, because a leaf may write its run id string in run.
            const continuedFromRun = new Set(rows
              .filter((row) => row && row.type === "iteration")
              .map((row) => (Number.isInteger(row.iteration) ? row.iteration : Number(row.run)))
              .filter((run) => Number.isInteger(run))).size;
            const addedIterations = Number(extendRaw);
            const priorMaxIterations = Number(config.maxIterations) || 0;
            const priorMaxDurationMinutes = Number(config.maxDurationMinutes) || 0;
            const createdMs = Date.parse(config.createdAt);
            const elapsedMinutesAtExtension = Number.isFinite(createdMs)
              ? Math.max(0, Math.floor((Date.now() - createdMs) / 60000))
              : 0;
            // An unset flag renders as an empty or unexpanded value, never as digits.
            const hasMinutes = /^[1-9][0-9]*$/.test(String(extendMinutesRaw));
            const addedMinutes = hasMinutes ? Number(extendMinutesRaw) : priorMaxDurationMinutes;
            // A run with no duration budget keeps none unless --extend-minutes asks for one.
            const newMaxDurationMinutes = priorMaxDurationMinutes > 0 || hasMinutes
              ? Math.max(priorMaxDurationMinutes, elapsedMinutesAtExtension) + addedMinutes
              : priorMaxDurationMinutes;
            const newMaxIterations = continuedFromRun + addedIterations;
            const sessionId = config.sessionId;
            const generation = Number(config.generation) || 1;
            const event = {
              stem: "deep_review.run_extended",
              scope: { runId: sessionId, sessionId, generation },
              data: {
                priorTailDigest: crypto.createHash("sha256").update(stateBytes).digest("hex"),
                continuedFromRun,
                extension: {
                  addedIterations,
                  priorMaxIterations,
                  newMaxIterations,
                  priorMaxDurationMinutes,

.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/references/protocol/loop-protocol.md:258:Iteration: {N} of {maxIterations}
.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/references/protocol/loop-protocol.md:459:Under `--depth-mode=expansive` the loop keeps a frontier of sub-target nodes beside the review target. The flag writes `antiConvergence.depthMode: "expansive"` and the caps in `expansiveDepth`: `maxDepth` 3, `maxChildrenPerNode` 6, a `budgetSplit` of 0.5/0.35/0.15 across the depth bands, and `maxFrontierNodes`, set to `maxIterations` at config creation. A run without the flag keeps the default path and runs exactly as before, and both the mode and the caps are locked against the optimizer.
.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/references/protocol/loop-protocol.md:469:On a fan-out run the depth-1 nodes are dealt round-robin in node-id order, `frontier_scope:` binds each lineage's node ids beside `review_focus:` in its prompt, and the partition is recorded as `frontier_partition` in `orchestration-summary.json`. Each lineage's store is written before it spawns, the merge returns a merged `frontier`, and a partition guard names any dealt node no lineage reports. A node needs one iteration per configured dimension, so 50% of each lineage's `maxIterations` should reach `ceil(nodes / lineages) * dimensions`; below that the depth-1 band cannot fund the nodes it was dealt.
.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/references/protocol/loop-protocol.md:625:2. Sets `maxIterations` to `continuedFromRun + N`, so a run stopped at 7 of 10 and extended by 3 ends at 10.
.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/references/protocol/quick-reference.md:161:**Key defaults:** `maxIterations=7`, `convergenceThreshold=0.10`, `rollingStopThreshold=0.08`, `noProgressThreshold=0.05`, `stuckThreshold=2`, `minStabilizationPasses=1`
.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/SKILL.md:229:`runtime/scripts/convergence.cjs` computes the whole stop decision once per pass from the config, the state log, the findings registry and the coverage graph. `deep-review.yaml` renders no stop arithmetic of its own. The frozen stop reasons are `converged`, `maxIterationsReached`, `blockedStop`, `manualStop`, `error`, `stuckRecovery`, `userPaused` and `maxDurationReached`. A missing graph decision blocks every non-terminal STOP.

    label: 'b',
    registry: registryWithFrontier([frontierNode('F-1-002', 'answered'), frontierNode('F-2-003', 'open')]),
  };

  const withoutFrontier = ({ label, registry }: LineageDataFixture): LineageDataFixture => {
    const { frontier: _frontier, ...rest } = registry as Record<string, unknown>;
    return { label, registry: rest };
  };

  it('merges each lineage frontier with its label and stats', () => {
    const merged = mergeReviewRegistries([lineageA, lineageB]);
    const frontier = merged.frontier as {
      nodes: Array<{ nodeId: string; lineageNodeId: string; lineage: string }>;
      stats: Record<string, number>;
    };
    expect(frontier.nodes).toHaveLength(4);
    expect(frontier.nodes.filter((node) => node.lineageNodeId === 'F-2-003').map((node) => node.nodeId)).toEqual(['a:F-2-003', 'b:F-2-003']);
    expect(frontier.stats).toMatchObject({ total: 4, open: 2, exhausted: 1, answered: 1 });
  });

  it('passes the guard when every partitioned node is reported', () => {
    const merged = mergeReviewRegistries([lineageA, lineageB], {
      frontierPartition: { a: ['F-1-001'], b: ['F-1-002'] },
    });
    const frontier = merged.frontier as { partitionGuard?: { passed: boolean; missing: string[] } };
    expect(frontier.partitionGuard).toEqual({ passed: true, missing: [] });
  });

  it('fails the guard when a partitioned node is missing from every lineage', () => {
    const merged = mergeReviewRegistries([lineageA, lineageB], {
      frontierPartition: { a: ['F-1-001', 'F-1-004'], b: ['F-1-002'] },
    });
    const frontier = merged.frontier as { partitionGuard?: { passed: boolean; missing: string[] } };
    expect(frontier.partitionGuard).toEqual({ passed: false, missing: ['F-1-004'] });
  });

  it('gives every merged node a unique id when lineages number their children alike', () => {
    // Each lineage numbers the nodes it admits from its own store, so three lineages that
    // each hold one dealt depth-1 node all name their first child F-2-002.
    const lineage = (label: string, parentId: string) => ({
      label,
      registry: registryWithFrontier([
        { nodeId: parentId, parentId: 'root', status: 'answered' } as { nodeId: string; status: string },
        { nodeId: 'F-2-002', parentId, status: 'pruned' } as { nodeId: string; status: string },
      ]),
    });
    const merged = mergeReviewRegistries([lineage('pi-2', 'F-1-076'), lineage('pi-3', 'F-1-077'), lineage('pi-4', 'F-1-078')], {
      frontierPartition: { 'pi-2': ['F-1-076'], 'pi-3': ['F-1-077'], 'pi-4': ['F-1-078'] },
    });
    const frontier = merged.frontier as {
      nodes: Array<{ nodeId: string; lineageNodeId: string; parentId: string; lineage: string }>;
      partitionGuard?: { passed: boolean; missing: string[] };
    };
    const ids = frontier.nodes.map((node) => node.nodeId);

    expect(new Set(ids).size).toBe(ids.length);
    const child = frontier.nodes.find((node) => node.lineage === 'pi-3' && node.lineageNodeId === 'F-2-002');
    expect(child).toBeDefined();
    expect(ids).toContain(child?.parentId);
    expect(frontier.partitionGuard).toEqual({ passed: true, missing: [] });
  });

  it('adds no frontier key when no review lineage has one', () => {
    const merged = mergeReviewRegistries([withoutFrontier(lineageA), withoutFrontier(lineageB)]);
    expect(Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(merged, 'frontier')).toBe(false);
  });
});

# DRV-068 -- Cross-mode anti-convergence contract

This document captures the realistic user-testing contract, current behavior, execution flow, source anchors, and metadata for `DRV-068`.

---

## 1. OVERVIEW

This scenario validates the `deep-review` slice of the cross-mode anti-convergence contract. The objective is to confirm that review mode declares a minimum iteration floor, exposes a fail-closed runtime stop policy, resolves capabilities through the shared runtime helper, and protects the contract from optimizer drift.

### WHY THIS MATTERS

Review loops can produce convincing but incomplete early STOP decisions when only one pass has run. The anti-convergence floor makes that early stop illegal unless the configured minimum has been satisfied, while the runtime and optimizer guards keep capability loading and tuning from weakening the stop policy.

---

## 2. SCENARIO CONTRACT

Operators should run this as a deterministic documentation-and-config check against the shipped files.

- Objective: Verify `deep-review` has `antiConvergence.minIterations: 2`, `convergenceMode: "default"`, `stopPolicy: "fail-closed"`, a runtime capability matrix with fail-closed stop policy, and optimizer invariant locks for convergence mode plus `minIterations <= maxIterations`.
- Real user request: Check whether deep-review can stop after one iteration or tune around its anti-convergence floor.
- Prompt: `Validate the deep-review anti-convergence floor, fail-closed stop policy, runtime capability resolver, and optimizer invariant guard.`
- Expected execution process: Inspect the review config and runtime capability matrix, then inspect the shared runtime resolver and optimizer manifest.
- Desired user-facing outcome: The user understands that review mode declares a 2-iteration floor, fails closed when runtime stop policy is missing or wrong, and rejects optimizer candidates where the floor exceeds `maxIterations`.
- Expected signals: `antiConvergence.minIterations` equals `2`; `stopPolicy` equals `fail-closed`; `runtime-capabilities.cjs` rejects missing or non-fail-closed stop policy; optimizer manifest locks convergence mode and carries `minIterations<=maxIterations`.
- Pass/fail posture: PASS if all four anchors agree. FAIL if any anchor is missing, permissive, or contradictory.

---

## 3. TEST EXECUTION

### RECOMMENDED ORCHESTRATION PROCESS

1. Restate the user request in plain language.
2. Follow the command sequence in order.
3. Capture grep outputs for every expected signal.
4. Return a concise verdict with any drift called out explicitly.

### Prompt

Validate the deep-review anti-convergence floor, fail-closed stop policy, runtime capability resolver, and optimizer invariant guard.

### Commands

1. `bash: rg -n '"antiConvergence"|"minIterations": 2|"convergenceMode": "default"|"stopPolicy": "fail-closed"' .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/assets/deep-review-config.json`
2. `bash: rg -n '"stopPolicy": "fail-closed"' .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/assets/runtime-capabilities.json`
3. `bash: rg -n 'missing stopPolicy|stopPolicy must be "fail-closed"|validateStopPolicy' .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/lib/deep-loop/runtime-capabilities.cjs`
4. `bash: rg -n 'antiConvergence.convergenceMode|convergenceMode|minIterations<=maxIterations|minIterations|maxIterations' .skilled/skills/system-spec-kit/runtime/cli/optimizer/optimizer-manifest.json`

### Expected

The config declares the review floor and fail-closed policy, the runtime matrix repeats fail-closed policy, the shared resolver rejects missing or invalid stop policy, and the optimizer manifest locks convergence mode while requiring `minIterations <= maxIterations`.

### Evidence

Capture the matching lines from the four command outputs.

### Pass/Fail

PASS if all commands return the expected anchors with no contradictory values. FAIL if `minIterations`, `stopPolicy`, resolver validation, or optimizer invariant metadata is absent or permissive.

### Failure Triage

Privilege the live config and runtime helper over older references. If the optimizer manifest path changes, use `rg --files .skilled/skills | rg 'optimizer-manifest\.json$'` and rerun the invariant check against the discovered path.

.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/README.md:127:`--depth-mode=expansive` lets a run grow its own questions. A seed step writes 3 to 6 depth-1 nodes before iteration 1, and the leaf may propose children only when its prompt carries a frontier block. An admission step before the reducer applies six checks in order, relevance, dedupe, depth, fan-out, budget and cycle, and prunes the proposal on the first failure. Next Focus reads the shallowest open node first and writes it as a frontier line, so visiting a node does not read as answering it. While a node stays open the frontier vetoes a soft convergence stop, which never outranks the iteration cap or the other hard stops, and the caps are `maxDepth` 3, `maxChildrenPerNode` 6, a `0.5/0.35/0.15` budget split and `maxFrontierNodes`.

=== review SKILL

The stuck count and the consecutive failure count are rebuilt from the state log on every pass, so a resumed run counts the same as one that never paused. Failed iterations stay out of the ratio signals. Three trailing failed iterations stop the loop with stop reason `error`. In confirm mode only, the workflow first asks the operator Retry, Skip or Cancel. Auto mode has no such question.

Each pass also compares `maxDurationMinutes` with the config `createdAt`. When the limit is reached the loop skips the dispatch and goes to synthesis with stop reason `maxDurationReached`.

### Expansive Depth Mode

- `--depth-mode=expansive` keeps a depth-capped frontier of sub-target nodes beside the review target. An absent flag means `default`, which runs exactly as before.
- The flag writes `antiConvergence.depthMode` and the caps in `expansiveDepth`: `maxDepth` 3, `maxChildrenPerNode` 6, a `0.5/0.35/0.15` budget split across the depth bands, and `maxFrontierNodes`, set to `maxIterations` at config creation. Both are locked against the optimizer.
- `step_frontier_seed` writes the depth-1 nodes before iteration 1 through `runtime/scripts/frontier-admit.cjs seed --target review`, after `step_create_config` or before `step_fanout_spawn` on a fan-out run. A merge with no diff of its own is pruned `merge-no-own-diff` and an index rebuild `generated-artifact`.
- `step_frontier_admit` runs before the reducer and prunes a `frontier_proposal` on the first failed check among relevance, dedupe, depth, fan-out, budget and cycle. Node status lives in `review/frontier-nodes.jsonl`, written only by `frontier-admit.cjs`; review has no inbox.
- `step_read_state` reads the active node and sets `next_dimension` to its next uncovered dimension, `step_frontier_activate` marks it active before dispatch, and the prompt pack's `{frontier_block}` names the node. Next Focus takes the blocked stop, then the pivot, then the node, then the leaf focus, then the first uncovered dimension.
- While a node stays open the frontier veto turns a stop candidate into `CONTINUE` with reasonDetail `frontierOpen`. It never outranks the iteration cap, consecutive failures, stuck recovery, pause, `--convergence-mode=off`, `--stop-policy=max-iterations` or the review floor, and stuck recovery closes the active node `exhausted`.
- A fan-out run deals the depth-1 nodes round-robin in node-id order, binds `frontier_scope:` beside `review_focus:`, records the partition as `frontier_partition` in `orchestration-summary.json` and returns a partition guard for any node no lineage reports. Size `--max-iterations` so half of it covers `ceil(nodes / lineages) * dimensions`.

81:`research/frontier-nodes.jsonl` is the node-status store for a run started with `--depth-mode=expansive`. A default run never creates it. | Mode | Live Behavior |
|------|---------------|
| `default` | Uses normal full-history convergence signals and sends a legal STOP to synthesis and verdict derivation. This is the fallback when no mode is supplied. |
| `off` | Is accepted and persisted, but the shipped review YAML has no `off` decision branch; review therefore follows the same legal-stop and synthesis path as `default`. Use `stopPolicy: "max-iterations"` when convergence must remain telemetry-only until the iteration ceiling. |
| `sliding-window` | Is accepted and persisted, but windowed graph-novelty telemetry is research-only in the shared runtime; review therefore retains its ordinary full-history signals, legal-stop gates, and synthesis path. |
| `divergent` | After all review gates pass, translates the eligible `all_dimensions_clean` STOP into a read-only three-seat direction-selection pivot and continues with the selected review direction. |

#### Expansive Depth Mode

`--depth-mode=expansive` grows a depth-capped frontier of sub-target nodes beside the review target. The value lands in `antiConvergence.depthMode`, and the caps live in `expansiveDepth`: `maxDepth` 3, `maxChildrenPerNode` 6, a `budgetSplit` of 0.5/0.35/0.15 across the depth bands, and `maxFrontierNodes`, which is set to `maxIterations` when the config is created. Both the mode and the block are locked against the optimizer. An absent flag means `default`, which runs exactly as before.

After the review floor, an expansive run that still holds an open or active node turns a stop candidate into `CONTINUE` with reasonDetail `frontierOpen`, whichever candidate it was, `all_dimensions_clean` or a composite convergence vote. The veto never creates a stop of its own and never outranks the iteration cap, consecutive failures, stuck recovery, pause, `--convergence-mode=off`, `--stop-policy=max-iterations` or the `minIterations` floor. Under stuck recovery the handler closes the active node as `exhausted` with reason `stuck`, so a node the run cannot fund stops holding the veto open.

=== 605-613
### Locked Fields (Not Optimizer-Tunable)

The following fields are runtime contracts and MUST NOT be modified by the optimizer:

- `stopReason` enum values and semantics
- `blocked_stop` event structure and gate names
- Depth mode (`antiConvergence.depthMode`) and the `expansiveDepth` caps
- Lineage fields (`sessionId`, `lineageMode`, `generation`)
- Reducer configuration and file protection policies
- Review dimensions (`reviewDimensions`) and product mode (`mode`)

# DRV-075 -- Covered custom dimension reaches the all-dimensions-clean stop

This document captures the realistic user-testing contract, current behavior, execution flow, source anchors, and metadata for `DRV-075`.

---

## 1. OVERVIEW

This scenario validates the clean-stop gate for a custom review dimension for `DRV-075`. It focuses on a review run that names `performance` next to the four canonical dimensions: once `performance` is covered, the convergence check must stop the run with `all_dimensions_clean`, and the review pivot must not hold a candidate for `performance` open.

### Why This Matters

A custom dimension that the reducer tracks but never marks covered keeps the stop gate open for the whole run. The run then reaches no clean stop, and the pivot seeds an `unswept-dimension` candidate that never closes. The operator sees a review that never finishes even though every configured dimension was checked.

---

## 2. SCENARIO CONTRACT

Operators run this as a convergence check on a review loop state that covers the custom dimension. The outcome must come from the coverage the reducer records, not from the default four-dimension path.

- Objective: Confirm that a configured custom dimension, once covered, lets the review stop with `all_dimensions_clean`, and that the pivot seeds no `unswept-dimension` candidate for it.
- Real user request: I added performance to the review. Once a pass has checked it, the review should finish cleanly and not keep waiting on it.
- Prompt: `Run a deep review with review_dimensions: correctness,performance, cover performance on one iteration, and confirm the run stops with all_dimensions_clean rather than holding open on a missing performance dimension.`
- Expected execution process: Run the convergence integration case that records a custom dimension as covered and checks the stop reason, then run the review pivot case for the same covered dimension, then read the line in the convergence script that sets the clean-stop reason.
- Expected signals: The convergence case stops clean with `performance` covered, and its stop decision does not list `performance` as missing. The pivot case accepts the clean-stop trigger and seeds no `unswept-dimension` candidate for `performance`. The convergence script sets the `all_dimensions_clean` reason on the clean-stop candidate.
- Desired user-visible outcome: The operator is told the review finished because every configured dimension, including performance, was covered.
- Pass/fail: PASS if the convergence case and the pivot case pass and the clean-stop line is present; FAIL if the run holds open on `performance`, stops with a different reason, or the pivot seeds an `unswept-dimension` candidate for `performance`.

---

## 3. TEST EXECUTION

### Prompt

- Prompt: `Run a deep review with review_dimensions: correctness,performance, cover performance on one iteration, and confirm the run stops with all_dimensions_clean rather than holding open on a missing performance dimension.`

### Commands

1. `cd .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime && npx vitest run review-depth-convergence.vitest -t "custom dimension"`
2. `cd .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime && npx vitest run divergent-pivot.vitest -t "custom dimension"`
3. `rg -n "reasonDetail: 'all_dimensions_clean'" .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/scripts/convergence.cjs`

### Expected

Command 1 exits 0 with `stops clean when the reducer records the configured custom dimension as covered` reported as passed and no failed test. Command 2 exits 0 with `accepts the clean-stop trigger and seeds no unswept-dimension for a covered custom dimension` reported as passed and no failed test. Command 3 prints one line, the clean-stop candidate assignment that sets `reasonDetail` to `all_dimensions_clean`.

### Evidence

Capture the pass lines from command 1 and command 2 with their case names, and the command 3 line that sets the clean-stop reason.

### Pass / Fail

- **Pass**: Commands 1 and 2 exit 0 with their named cases passing, and command 3 prints the clean-stop assignment.
- **Fail**: A named case fails, the run stops with a reason other than `all_dimensions_clean` once `performance` is covered, the pivot seeds an `unswept-dimension` for `performance`, or command 3 prints nothing.

### Failure Triage

If command 1 fails, check that the reducer marks the configured custom name as covered in the dimension coverage map, since the stop gate reads that map and the canonical four alone do not satisfy it. If command 2 fails, check the pivot's unswept-dimension candidates for the covered custom name, since a covered dimension must not seed one. If command 3 prints nothing, the clean-stop assignment has moved, so find the new location of `all_dimensions_clean` in `convergence.cjs` before judging the stop.

### Optional Supplemental Checks

- Whole-file stop path: run `cd .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime && npx vitest run review-depth-convergence.vitest` without a name filter. It must exit 0 with no failed test, so the canonical stop cases in the same file still pass beside the custom-dimension case.

[0m$ [0mcd /Users/michelkerkmeester/worktrees/public/455-expansive-depth-integration && sed -n '253,258p' README.md; echo "=== feature catalog"; sed -n '45,56p' .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/feature-catalog/feature-catalog.md; echo "=== gate"; grep -n "gate_results_array_json:" .skilled/commands/deep/assets/deep-review.yaml
Each pass runs in a fresh context against state kept on disk. A script, not the agent, decides when to stop. A review stays open while a P0 is active or a required check still fails, and it ends in a PASS, CONDITIONAL or FAIL verdict. A loop survives crashes and new sessions, because its progress lives in files.

Two opt-in flags let a loop reach further. `--depth-mode=expansive` keeps a frontier of sub-questions or sub-targets and turns a stop into another pass while one stays open, though never past the iteration cap. `--extend=N` adds N passes to a finished single-executor run in the same session. A review can also track custom dimensions beside its four defaults.

A loop can also spread across several AI CLIs at once. Each executor runs its own lineage, and for a review the strictest verdict wins the merge. A pool caps how many lineages run at once. A stall watchdog stops a lineage that stops making progress, and a cost cap bounds what each one spends. The [Deep Loop README](.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/README.md) describes both modes and the shared runtime.

=== feature catalog
#### Description

The --depth-mode flag on /deep:research and /deep:review selects default or expansive depth, and leaves the registry-driven workflowMode, runtimeLoopType and backendKind classification unchanged.

#### How It Works

`mode-registry.json` has no depth field, and the runtime acts only on the expansive value. The behavior is documented in the research and review catalogs.

#### Source Files

See [`deep-loop-mode-classification/depth-mode-flag.md`](deep-loop-mode-classification/depth-mode-flag.md) for full implementation and validation file listings.

# Initialization

## 1. OVERVIEW

Sets up the deep research packet, canonical state files, and lineage mode before the loop begins.

Initialization is the workflow-owned entry into a research session. It creates the packet directory, seeds the state surfaces that the LEAF agent will read later, and decides whether the run is a fresh start, a resume, or a restart.

---

## 2. HOW IT WORKS

The live initialization contract classifies the packet state before it writes anything. It distinguishes fresh state, resumable state, completed-session state, and invalid state. Fresh runs create `research/deep-research-config.json`, the first JSONL line in `research/deep-research-state.jsonl`, `research/deep-research-strategy.md`, and `research/findings-registry.json`. Confirm mode adds a charter review gate before the first iteration continues.

Lineage handling is narrower than some older drafts. The runtime supports `new`, `resume`, `restart`, and `completed-continue`. Resume keeps the same session identifier and appends a typed `resumed` event. Restart archives the old research tree, mints a fresh session identifier, increments generation, and appends a typed `restarted` event. `completed-continue` is live through `--extend=N` on a completed run: it keeps the session identifier and generation, sets `maxIterations` to the iterations run so far plus N, appends a typed `run_extended` event, and copies the prior synthesis to a versioned snapshot before the first new iteration.

`fork` remains deferred and is not exposed as a live mode.

---

## 3. SOURCE FILES

### Implementation

| File | Layer | Role |
|---|---|---|
| `.skilled/commands/deep/research.md` | Command | Defines the init phase inputs, packet outputs, and setup contract before the YAML workflow loads. |
| `.skilled/commands/deep/assets/deep-research.yaml` | Workflow | Creates canonical state files on the initialization path. Confirm mode adds charter review and state checks. |
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/references/protocol/loop-protocol.md` | Reference | Documents session classification, canonical names, and the resumed or restarted lifecycle event contract. |
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/assets/deep-research-config.json` | Asset | Supplies the default config shape written during initialization. |
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/assets/deep-research-strategy.md` | Asset | Supplies the initial strategy structure and anchor layout. |

### Validation And Tests

| File | Type | Role |
|---|---|---|

| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/manual-testing-playbook/initialization-and-state-setup/resume-classification-from-valid-prior-state.md` | Manual playbook | Verifies resume classification and restart-safe continuation from valid state. |
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/manual-testing-playbook/initialization-and-state-setup/invalid-or-contradictory-state-halts-for-repair.md` | Manual playbook | Verifies contradictory packet state halts instead of guessing. |
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/manual-testing-playbook/initialization-and-state-setup/research-charter-validation.md` | Manual playbook | Verifies the research charter sections exist before the loop proceeds. |

=== depth-mode-flag
---
title: "Depth mode flag (--depth-mode)"
description: "The --depth-mode flag on /deep:research and /deep:review selects default or expansive depth, and leaves the registry-driven workflowMode, runtimeLoopType and backendKind classification unchanged."
trigger_phrases:
  - "--depth-mode"
  - "depth mode flag"
  - "expansive depth flag"
  - "deep loop depth mode"
version: 1.0.1.0
---

# Depth mode flag (--depth-mode)

## 1. OVERVIEW

`--depth-mode` is a command-level option on `/deep:research` and `/deep:review`. It takes `default` or `expansive`. The flag is not part of `mode-registry.json`, so the hub classifies a request into the same workflow mode with or without it. The behavior the flag turns on lives in the research and review packets.

---

## 2. HOW IT WORKS

### Hub Classification Unchanged

`mode-registry.json` carries the `workflowMode`, `runtimeLoopType` and `backendKind` discriminator for `research` and `review`, and it has no depth field. Routing resolves the same packet whether the flag is present or not.

### Flag Routing

The command workflow reads `--depth-mode` and binds it as `depth_mode`. Only the value `expansive` reaches the runtime. `runtime/scripts/convergence.cjs` adds `depthMode` to its stop-decision config only when the value is `expansive`, and `runtime/scripts/fanout-run.cjs` passes `depthMode` to fan-out lineages only when the value is `expansive`. A default run carries no depth key in either place.

### Packet Behavior

The expansive behavior is documented in the packet catalogs: [expansive depth mode for research](../../deep-research/feature-catalog/convergence/expansive-depth-mode.md) and [expansive depth mode for review](../../deep-review/feature-catalog/loop-lifecycle/expansive-depth-mode.md).

---

## 3. SOURCE FILES

### Implementation

| File | Layer | Role |
|---|---|---|
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/mode-registry.json` | Registry | Holds the discriminator for `research` and `review`, with no depth field. |
| `.skilled/commands/deep/research.md` | Command doc | The `--depth-mode` flag row for research. |
| `.skilled/commands/deep/review.md` | Command doc | The `--depth-mode` flag row for review. |
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/scripts/convergence.cjs` | Script | Adds `depthMode` to the stop-decision config only when expansive. |
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/scripts/fanout-run.cjs` | Script | Passes `depthMode` to lineages only when expansive. |

### Validation And Tests

| File | Type | Role |
|---|---|---|
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/manual-testing-playbook/entry-points-and-modes/expansive-depth-flag-and-default-identity.md` | Manual playbook | DR-068: the flag defaults to `default` and a default run matches the baseline. |
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/manual-testing-playbook/entry-points-and-modes/expansive-depth-flag-and-default-identity.md` | Manual playbook | DRV-076: the review flag defaults to `default` and a default run matches the baseline. |

### Expansive depth mode (--depth-mode=expansive)

#### Description

Opt-in --depth-mode=expansive on deep-review seeds depth-one sub-target nodes from the review target, keeps node status in review/frontier-nodes.jsonl, and holds a review stop while nodes stay open.

#### How It Works

Node status lives in `review/frontier-nodes.jsonl`, open nodes count as `searchDebt`, and the divergent pivot waits for a terminal frontier. The live pilot runs are still open, so no pilot result is recorded here.

#### Source Files

See [`loop-lifecycle/expansive-depth-mode.md`](loop-lifecycle/expansive-depth-mode.md) for full implementation and validation file listings.

fix(system-deep-loop): repair expansive review frontier defects from the pilot

A live expansive review pilot over a 400-commit range found four defects on
the frontier path. The root shared the per-node children cap, so a review seed
admitted six depth-1 sub-targets although the workflow promises three to eight;
the root cap is now the top of each loop's documented seed range (eight for
review, six for research) and never below the per-node cap, which stays as it
was. A commit-range target typed as a file list made the range string the only
allowed path, so every depth-2 proposal pruned as irrelevant; relevance now
intersects a range target with the files scope discovery recorded for it.
Merged frontier nodes kept lineage-local ids, so three lineages each reported
F-2-002; merged ids and parent links are now lineage-qualified, with the local
id kept beside them for the partition guard. The review docs now name the
frontier_partition key the runner records in orchestration-summary.json.

Every change sits on the expansive path; both depth-mode golden suites pass.

.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/feature-catalog/convergence/expansive-depth-mode.md:16:`--depth-mode` takes `default` or `expansive`. A run without the flag is unchanged. `expansive` turns on a research frontier: the run seeds depth-one nodes from the topic before iteration 1, admits leaf `frontier_proposal` rows as nodes under depth, fan-out, budget, dedupe and cycle caps, and holds a convergence stop while frontier nodes stay open. Two live research pilots ran on one topic, and both failed the criterion that a stop candidate forms before the iteration cap: each run stopped on `maxIterationsReached`. This page describes the shipped code and its tests.

## 2. Dashboard / Checkpoint Layout

### Deep Research
=== review defaults
   +-- Defaults: maxIterations=7, convergenceThreshold=0.10, antiConvergence.convergenceMode=`default`, antiConvergence.divergent=`{}`, stop_policy=`convergence`, lineage_mode=`auto`, config.executor.kind=`native`, config.executor.timeoutSeconds=900, config.resource_map.emit=`true`
> build · deepseek-v4.1-flash
[0m$ [0mcd /Users/michelkerkmeester/worktrees/public/455-expansive-depth-integration && sed -n '450,475p' .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/references/protocol/loop-protocol.md; echo "=== 615-635"; sed -n '615,635p' .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/references/protocol/loop-protocol.md
**Recovery evaluation**:
- If recovery iteration finds any new P0/P1 or materially advances required traceability coverage: recovery successful. Reset stuck count. Continue.
- If recovery iteration finds only P2 or nothing: recovery failed. If all dimensions are already covered, exit to synthesis. Otherwise, move to the next unreviewed dimension.

---

### Expansive Depth Frontier (opt-in)

Under `--depth-mode=expansive` the loop keeps a frontier of sub-target nodes beside the review target. The flag writes `antiConvergence.depthMode: "expansive"` and the caps in `expansiveDepth`: `maxDepth` 3, `maxChildrenPerNode` 6, a `budgetSplit` of 0.5/0.35/0.15 across the depth bands, and `maxFrontierNodes`, set to `maxIterations` at config creation. A run without the flag keeps the default path and runs exactly as before, and the mode is locked against the optimizer; the `expansiveDepth` caps are in neither its tunable nor its locked list.

`step_frontier_seed` decomposes the review target into depth-1 sub-target nodes before iteration 1, through `runtime/scripts/frontier-admit.cjs seed --target review`. On a plain run it sits in `phase_init` after `step_create_config`; on a fan-out run it runs before `step_fanout_spawn`. A merge that carries no diff of its own is pruned `merge-no-own-diff` and an index rebuild `generated-artifact`, because neither is reviewable work.

`step_read_state` reads `active_frontier_node` from `registry.frontier.activeNodeId` and sets `next_dimension` to that node's next uncovered dimension, so the iteration reviews depth the node has not spent yet. `step_frontier_activate` marks the node `active` before the dispatch, and the prompt pack's `{frontier_block}` names the node; a leaf proposes `frontier_proposal` rows only when its block is non-empty.

`step_frontier_admit` runs before the reducer and applies six checks in order: relevance, dedupe, depth, fan-out, budget, cycle. Relevance includes a node's scope sitting inside a file-list review target. The first failure prunes the proposal under that check's name. A review run has no inbox, so the store alone queues an admitted node, and `runtime/scripts/frontier-admit.cjs --target review` is its only writer.

Next Focus reads, in precedence order: blocked-stop recovery, the pivot, the active frontier node, the leaf's `nextFocus`, then the first uncovered dimension. The `frontierOpen` veto holds the loop at CONTINUE while a node stays open, and never outranks the iteration cap, consecutive failures, stuck recovery, pause, `--convergence-mode=off`, `--stop-policy=max-iterations` or the review floor. Stuck recovery closes the active node `exhausted` with reason `stuck` before recovery continues.

On a fan-out run the depth-1 nodes are dealt round-robin in node-id order, `frontier_scope:` binds each lineage's node ids beside `review_focus:` in its prompt, and the partition is recorded as `frontier_partition` in `orchestration-summary.json`. Each lineage's store is written before it spawns, the merge returns a merged `frontier`, and a partition guard names any dealt node no lineage reports. A node needs one iteration per configured dimension, so 50% of each lineage's `maxIterations` should reach `ceil(nodes / lineages) * dimensions`; below that the depth-1 band cannot fund the nodes it was dealt.

---

## 4. PHASE 3: SYNTHESIS

### Purpose
=== 615-635
| `new` | fresh | 1 | n/a | implicit (config record) | no existing state |
| `resume` | same | same | none | `resumed` (see example above) | operator wants to continue the current lineage boundary |
| `restart` | fresh | `prior + 1` | prior `review/` tree moved under `review-archive/{timestamp}/` | `restarted` (same field set plus non-null `archivedPath`) | operator wants to clear the workspace and replay with a new angle |
| `completed-continue` | same | same | none; the prior review report is copied to `review/review-report-v{generation}-r{continuedFromRun}.md` | `extended` (see below) | a completed run needs N more iterations: pass `--extend=N` |

#### Completed-run extension

`--extend=N` (and optionally `--extend-minutes=M`) continues a run whose config has `status: "complete"`. Under the loop lock and before session classification, `step_extend_completed_run`:

1. Counts `continuedFromRun` as the distinct runs among the iteration records, error and timeout rows included.
2. Sets `maxIterations` to `continuedFromRun + N`, so a run stopped at 7 of 10 and extended by 3 ends at 10.
3. Sets `maxDurationMinutes` to `max(prior budget, minutes elapsed since createdAt) + M`. `M` defaults to the prior budget, and a run with no duration budget keeps none unless `--extend-minutes` is passed. `createdAt` is never re-stamped, because the duration check counts wall-clock time, idle time included.
4. Appends `deep_review.run_extended` through the append gateway, which projects an `extended` row into the state log, and only then replaces the config with `status: "initialized"`, `lineageMode: "completed-continue"` and `continuedFromRun`. A failed append leaves the run complete and untouched.

Classification then lands on resume. The workflow writes no `resumed` row for an extension, and the reducers treat an `extended` row like `resumed`, so the run is live again. Before the first new dispatch, `step_snapshot_extended_review_report` copies the prior review report to `review/review-report-v{generation}-r{continuedFromRun}.md` and refuses to overwrite an existing snapshot, so a second extension of the same generation keeps its own copy. `--extend` refuses a run that is not complete (use `--lineage-mode=resume`), refuses fan-out before any lineage spawns, and accepts positive integers only.

The extended row carries `{"type":"event","event":"extended","sessionId","generation","lineageMode":"completed-continue","continuedFromRun","extension","timestamp"}`, where `extension` holds `addedIterations`, `priorMaxIterations`, `newMaxIterations`, `priorMaxDurationMinutes`, `newMaxDurationMinutes` and `elapsedMinutesAtExtension`. It has no `archivedPath` or `parentSessionId`, so the field rule below covers `resumed` and `restarted` only.

**Convergence window.** Under `stopPolicy: convergence`, an extension with nothing new to cover re-stops on `all_dimensions_clean` at the first convergence check. The extension step prints a warning when this is likely; to keep the run going, pass `--convergence-mode=off` or use `stopPolicy: max-iterations`.

**Consecutive failures.** A run that completed on consecutive failures re-stops at the consecutive-failure check even under `--convergence-mode=off`, because that check runs before the `off` branch and the trailing failed iterations are still in the log.

 ### Decision Priority
 
-Checks are evaluated in this order (first match wins):
-
-1. **Max iterations** -- hard cap, always checked first.
-2. **All dimensions covered + clean** -- every configured dimension covered (the four by default), no active P0/P1, stabilization passed, gates passed.
-3. **Stuck detection** -- 2+ consecutive no-progress iterations.
-4. **Composite convergence** -- 3-signal weighted vote with threshold 0.60.
-5. **Legal-stop gate bundle** -- review-specific stop gates. If a stop vote fails any gate, persist `stopReason=blockedStop` and continue.

+Checks are evaluated in this order (first match wins), as `buildStopDecision` in `runtime/scripts/convergence.cjs` runs them:
+
+1. **Max iterations** -- hard cap, always checked first. It outranks every veto below.
+2. **Consecutive failures** -- three trailing failed iterations stop the run with stop reason `error`.
+3. **Stuck detection** -- `stuckThreshold` (2) consecutive no-progress iterations route to stuck recovery.
+4. **Convergence mode off** -- `convergenceMode: off` returns `CONTINUE` (`convergenceModeOff`).
+5. **Stop policy max-iterations** -- `stopPolicy: max-iterations` returns `CONTINUE` (`stopPolicyMaxIterations`).
+6. **Stop candidate** -- all dimensions covered and clean (every configured dimension covered, the four by default, no active P0/P1, coverage stabilized) is a hard candidate; otherwise the 3-signal composite vote with threshold 0.60 is a soft one. No candidate returns `CONTINUE` (`noStopCandidate`).
+7. **Iteration floor** -- below `minIterations` the candidate returns `CONTINUE` (`minIterationsNotReached`).
+8. **Frontier veto** -- under `--depth-mode=expansive`, an open frontier node turns the candidate into `CONTINUE` (`frontierOpen`).
+9. **Legal-stop gate bundle** -- review-specific stop gates. If the candidate fails any gate, persist `stopReason=blockedStop` and continue.
+10. **Graph decision** -- the candidate is combined with the graph convergence verdict: `STOP_ALLOWED` stops, `STOP_BLOCKED` blocks the stop and `CONTINUE` continues the run.

## 3. TEST EXECUTION

### Prerequisites

- Working directory is the repository root.
- The `runtime/` vitest suites are installed under `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime`.

### Prompt

- Prompt: `Check that an expansive deep-review run writes its depth-one sub-target nodes before iteration one, on a plain run and on a fan-out run, and that the seed creates no inbox file. Return a PASS or FAIL verdict with the reason.`

### Commands

1. `cd .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime && npx vitest run frontier-admit-review.vitest -t "seeds a review run" --reporter=verbose`
2. `cd .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime && npx vitest run stop-decision-yaml-contract.vitest -t "seeds the review frontier before fan-out spawns|seeds the single-executor review frontier right after the config exists" --reporter=verbose`
3. `rg -n "step_frontier_seed:" .skilled/commands/deep/assets/deep-review.yaml`

### Expected

Command 1 prints `Tests  1 passed | 22 skipped (23)`, and the case `seeds a review run, settling a candidate that carries its own prune reason` passes. That case asserts two open rows at depth one and no inbox file. Command 2 prints `Tests  2 passed | 32 skipped (34)`, with the fan-out placement case and the single-executor placement case both passing. Command 3 prints two lines: line 319, the fan-out seed step that runs before any lineage spawns, and line 898, the single-executor seed step that runs right after the config is created so iteration one already has open nodes.

### Evidence

- The verbose case lines and summary lines from commands 1 and 2, with each exit status.
- The two `rg` lines from command 3, read from the current workflow.

### Pass / Fail

- **Pass**: Commands 1 and 2 exit 0 with the expected counts, and command 3 prints both seed steps.
- **Fail**: A seeded node has no depth, an `inbox.jsonl` file appears, a placement case fails, or command 3 prints no seed step.

### Failure Triage

1. Command 1 fails on a row count or a depth: check that the seed writes its rows at depth one, with the parent set to the root.
